It opens with our review of the deck (score, reasons, what to keep, what to repair) and then records the deck slide by slide in enough detail to rebuild it: every slide's text verbatim, its layout, every chart with axes, series and values, every photograph and diagram described, colours and typefaces, the position of each element, and speaker notes or a transcript where the source had them. ## Review

**6 out of 10, Needs substantial work.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a strong, consistent identity (dark maze pattern, coral red, light sans) and a keynote rhythm of big statements and big numbers that would read well from a stage. Execution faults show up repeatedly in the pages I could read: product labels are broken mid-word and cramped in the boxes (slide 10 'Programma/ble'), the 'Developers' footnote runs off the edge of slide 7, the title and blocks collide on slide 10, and the add-on slides split words ('NUM/BER', 'RECO/RDIN/G') and truncate labels. Slide 124 lays full-size text over a dimmed hub diagram, so both are hard to read, and the Uber, ING and add-ons stretches are mostly text-only without much evidence on screen. Much of the argument depends on the speaker, and the deck on its own leaves the audience with sparse content and several visibly unfinished layouts.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the big-number metric slides (7 to 9), the stacked building-blocks build from 2008 to 2016 (slides 49 to 56), the consistent maze pattern with coral accents, and the one-line statement slides.

**Most useful repair:** Fix the broken word wraps and clipped labels on slides 7, 10 and the add-ons slides (59, 60, 62 to 66), and separate the text from the diagram on slide 124.

**What would change the score:** Make the add-on and messaging-hub diagrams readable at size with larger tiles and unbroken labels, and make the ING, Uber and Hall of Doers sections carry one concrete visual each.

_Confidence medium. 72 of 128 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-09. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

This deck is the keynote from Twilio Signal 2016, the company's developer conference. It opens with a Signal title card and a Twilio brand slide, followed by 'Hall of Doers' slides naming customer projects. Early metric slides report a million registered developer accounts, 75 billion annual API requests and over a billion global devices. A building-blocks slide lists the product lines, and a roadmap slide covers recent releases. The middle sections, each led by a different speaker, cover operating at scale and resilience, with reliability quotes and an operational maturity model. They also include a case study of contact-centre software built on Twilio products, and product talks on add-ons, Programmable Wireless with SIM, data and pricing, and the Notify preview. Later sections address conversational messaging, with a messaging hub diagram and the idea that every notification starts a conversation. The deck closes with 'Human after all' repeated as a closing title. Worth studying are the big-number metric slides, the maturity model diagram and the messaging hub build.

## Questions about this deck

**What does the Twilio Signal 2016 keynote deck say about how many registered developer accounts Twilio has?**

Slide 7 reports 1,000,000 developers, described as registered Twilio accounts as of May 23, 2016.

**What annual API request and global device figures does the Twilio deck give?**

Slide 8 gives 75 BILLION annual API requests and slide 9 says Over 1 BILLION global devices. Both slides cite Source: Twilio internal data.

**Which product lines does the Twilio deck list on its building blocks slide?**

Slide 10 lists building blocks including Programmable Voice, Programmable SMS, Lookup, Programmable Video, IP Messaging, Authy, Elastic SIP Trunking, Task Router, Network Traversal Service and Interconnect.

**How is Twilio's operational maturity model structured in the deck?**

Slide 19 presents the Operational Maturity Model with five areas: Security, Resilience, Build & Test, Architecture, and Operational Supportability.

### Slide 10

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The building-blocks grid turns the product portfolio into a compact, scannable diagram of tiles.
- Review: Execution faults show up repeatedly in the pages I could read: product labels are broken mid-word and cramped in the boxes (slide 10 'Programma/ble'), the 'Developers' footnote runs off the edge of slide 7, the title and blocks collide on slide 10, and the add-on slides split words ('NUM/BER', 'RECO/RDIN/G') and truncate labels.
- Suggested repair: Fix the broken word wraps and clipped labels on slides 7, 10 and the add-ons slides (59, 60, 62 to 66), and separate the text from the diagram on slide 124.

**Text**

BUILDING / BLOCKS / Programma / ble Voice / Programma / ble / SMS / Lookup / Programma / ble / Video / IP / Messaging / Authy / Elastic SIP / Trunking / Task Router / Network / Traversal / Service / Interconnect

**Layout**

Slide is a 16:9 area centred vertically in a letterboxed frame, with dark bars above and below. The title BUILDING / BLOCKS is stacked flush left at about 4% from the left edge in the upper-left quadrant. A 4-column by 3-row grid of red rectangles sits to the right of and below the title, starting around 32% from the left and 29% from the top, with the first row offset to columns 2 and 3. A thin horizontal rule runs near the bottom, about 94% down, from about 1% to 99% of the width. There is no footer text and no slide number.

**Visuals**

Twelve flat red rectangular labels arranged as a grid, with no icons, photos or charts. Row 1 (top): Programmable Voice in column 2 and Programmable SMS in column 3. Row 2: Lookup (column 1), Programmable Video (column 2), IP Messaging (column 3), Authy (column 4). Row 3: Elastic SIP Trunking (column 1), Task Router (column 2), Network Traversal Service (column 3), Interconnect (column 4). Each box is a solid coral-red rectangle with sharp square corners and no border or shadow, with white text centred inside. The only other visual is a thin grey horizontal rule. The dark background carries a faint, low-contrast diagonal chevron or maze pattern in slightly lighter slate.

**Design**

Background: Dark slate #262D35 with a faint diagonal chevron/maze pattern, inside dark charcoal #262626 letterbox bars above and below. Dark slate background with a faint tonal chevron pattern. Title text is white (#FFFFFF), set in a light geometric sans-serif (Avenir Light or Gotham Light style) in all caps with generous letter-spacing, roughly 20% of slide height per line. Box labels are white (#FFFFFF) light sans-serif, regular weight, mixed case, roughly 3.2% of slide height. Accent colour is coral red #FF4450 for the boxes. Rule is grey #6B7280 about 1 px thick. Boxes are sharp-cornered with no shadows or gradients.

No real title placeholder or grid; the footer name and page marker sit bottom right inside the photo, left-aligned to a line at about x 81.5%.

**Visuals**

Three black-and-white photographs, abutting with no gaps and no borders. (1) Upper-left photo, x 0-49%, y 12.4-50.5%: a young man in a light jacket on the left in profile with his arm raised and a wristband, a bearded man in a dark crew-neck sweater over a white collared shirt standing at centre-left, holding a beer bottle at his waist and smiling, a person with curly hair and glasses seen from behind at right, and a white-shirted figure in the foreground; background is a pale whiteboard wall; soft, even daylight; mid-greys and near-whites; cropped at the slide edges. (2) Lower-left photo, x 0-51%, y 50.5-87.5%: two young men seated at a table with an open laptop, the left in a dark quarter-zip jacket and the right in a light grey t-shirt printed with a line-art globe, a person standing at the far left edge in a dark zip fleece; a wooden cabinet and a pale wall behind; cropped at slide edges. (3) Right photo, x 49-100%, y 12.4-87.5%: a group of people seen from behind and in profile around a table, a woman in a striped top at right, a person with glasses, papers and a laptop on the table, shallow depth of field; the lower part fades to near-black; grey and charcoal tones; cropped at the right and bottom edges. A dark grey tint darkens the right photo overall. No charts, logos or icons.

**Design**

Background is charcoal #262626 in bands; the photos carry a neutral grey monochrome treatment. Title text is white #FFFFFF in a regular-weight humanist sans-serif similar to Open Sans, with no caps. '50' is the largest element at roughly 45% of slide height in cap size; 'Small' and 'Teams' are about 23% of slide height. Footer text is light grey #BFBFBF, small, uppercase, letter-spaced, sans-serif. Thin hairline rules are faintly visible along the lower photo edge. No corner radii, shadows or gradients.

### Slide 33

**Text**

more / Customers / more / Volume / more / Connectivity / more / Data / Lower Price / + / Higher Quality

**Layout**

A single tilted white paper napkin sits centred on the slide, its corners at roughly x 30 to 65 percent and y 21 to 75 percent. The napkin is rotated about 15 to 20 degrees and carries a hand-drawn cycle of four labelled nodes around an empty centre. A thin grey horizontal rule runs near the bottom at about 83 percent of the slide height, spanning almost the full width with margins of about 1.2 percent. There is no title, footer, page number or slide number. Dark bands fill the top (0 to 12.5 percent) and bottom (87.5 to 100 percent) of the image.

**Visuals**

Photograph of a white two-ply paper cocktail napkin lying flat on a dark background, photographed from above at a slight angle and rotated. Hand-drawn in black marker on the napkin: a clockwise loop of four labelled nodes with curved arrows. Top node reads Customers, with an arrow curving from it clockwise to the right, labelled more, and pointing down to the right node. Right node reads Volume, with two curved arrows descending from it toward the bottom-right, one ending at the node labelled more Connectivity and one ending at the node labelled more Data. Bottom node reads more Data, with an arrow curving left and up from it to the node at the lower left. Lower-left node reads Lower Price plus Higher Quality, with an arrow curving up from the left side of the loop to Customers, labelled more. The arrowheads are simple open chevrons. Lighting is soft and even, with a faint shadow under the napkin. Dominant colours are white, near-black ink and dark slate. The napkin is fully shown, not cropped, and is boxed only by the background. There is no overlay or tint on the napkin. The grey rule is a flat line about 1 pixel thick in light grey.

**Design**

### Slide 40

**Text**

Our enablers
ING Bank's strategy aims to create a differentiating customer experience by simplifying and streamlining our organisation, by further striving for operational excellence, by enhancing the performance culture within our company, and by expanding our lending capabilities.
Simplify and streamline
We need to be agile, with short reporting lines and with the right people empowered to make important decisions quickly.
Operational excellence
We need to set clear operational excellence benchmarks and measure performance throughout the company.
Performance culture
We need to excel at attracting and keeping the talented people we need to get us where we want to go.
Landing capabilities
We have strong savings gathering and lending capabilities, but savings and lending are not in balance in many of our business units. To achieve balanced growth, we need to develop our lending capabilities, particularly in areas such as consumer and SME lending.
9
ING

The year sits about 8% from the right edge and about 25% from the top; the label boxes sit centred horizontally at about 37% to 63% of width and 46% to 54% of height.

**Visuals**

Two red rectangular label boxes (flat, no shadow, square corners), each containing white text broken across three lines by word wrapping. Left box reads 'Programmable' / 'Voice'; right box reads 'Programmable' / 'SMS'. Behind everything is a subtle tonal geometric pattern of interlocking chevron/zigzag stripes in slightly lighter charcoal than the base, low contrast, covering the full slide except a darker band at top (about 0 to 12% height) and bottom (about 88% to 100%). No photographs, charts or logos.

**Design**

Background dark charcoal-navy #262C37 with a tonal chevron maze pattern in approximately #2C323E stripes and darker #232832 gaps. Year numeral and label text are white #FFFFFF. Accent boxes are red #FF4649. Typeface is a light humanist sans-serif similar to Gill Sans Light or Open Sans Light, regular-to-light weight, no italics, no capitals or letter-spacing. Year cap height is about 14% of slide height (font size about 19% of height). Label text is about 4.5% of slide height. Boxes have square corners, no border, no shadow, no gradient.

**Layout**

Four line-icon diagrams in white thin strokes with small white dots and circular white nodes, each under a red label. (1) FANOUT: a white circle containing a code icon '</>' at top centre, with dotted lines fanning down to five dots, each with a short vertical solid line hanging below. (2) FAILOVER: a small white circle with '</>' at left, a dotted arc rising from it to a triangle warning icon with an exclamation mark at right, with a curved arrow from the triangle to a dot at the far right. (3) LAST USED: a white circle with a double chevron/arrow glyph at left, with dotted lines fanning up-right to four dots and one circle with a check mark, and dotted lines fanning down-right to a dot. (4) CURRENT ACTIVE: a smaller version of the same diagram as LAST USED, with a white circle with double chevron at left, dotted lines fanning up to dots, a check-mark circle in the middle right, and a dot at bottom. Also a large white speech-bubble icon with a bold rounded-corner outline and a white exclamation mark, tail at lower left, to the left of NOTIFY.

**Design**

Background: Dark slate #232A31 with a subtle chevron maze pattern in #2A323A, inside a band from about 12.5% to 87.5% of height; charcoal #2B2B2B bars above and below. Title NOTIFY and subtitle ORCHESTRATIONS in white light geometric sans-serif (close to Open Sans Light or Lato Light), capitals, letter-spaced slightly. PREVIEW and the four category labels in coral red #E74C4C in the same light sans-serif. Icon strokes white #FFFFFF, about 2 px thick at this scale. Flat style, no shadows or gradients. Top and bottom bars are flat charcoal #2B2B2B.
